# Skills · Z · part 11

909 entries, zotero-refs to ztf-bs-popover-help-mobile. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **zotero-refs**: Add references to a Zotero library from PubMed via the Zotero Web API - resolve DOIs/citations to PMIDs, convert PubMed records to Zotero items matching what the browser connector produces, dedupe against the target library, and create them; then add new entries to an existing .bib without rewriting it. Use when asked to add, import, or sync references or a bibliography into Zotero, to get a manuscript's reference list into a Zotero group/collection, or to export or refresh a .bib from Zotero. ([rmgpanw/claude-skills](https://github.com/rmgpanw/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/zotero/skills/zotero-refs))
- **zotero-research**: Use when the user wants to search, browse, read, summarize, cite, annotate, or manage their Zotero library through Zotero MCP. Triggers include Zotero, 文献库, 查文献, 读论文, PDF 注释, citation key, BibTeX, 标签, 集合, and adding or updating Zotero items. ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/summer521521/Zotero_Research_plugin/skills/zotero-research) · ★1.6k · 2 copies)
- **zotero-research**: Spawnable research agent. Accepts bounded research requests, searches the indexed Zotero corpus through the deep-zotero MCP server, and returns claim-centred evidence cards with immediate verbatim passages, qualifications, contradictions, and search receipts. Callers spawn this via Task -- do not invoke directly. Never searches for, fetches, or imports external sources. ([ccam80/deep-zotero](https://github.com/ccam80/deep-zotero/tree/HEAD/examples/zotero-research) · ★9)
- **zotero-research**: Search and inspect the user's local Zotero library with OmniScholar. Use for collections, bibliographic metadata, notes, annotations, attachments, indexed text, local-paper matching, or selecting a PDF without modifying Zotero. ([luffysolution-svg/omnischolar](https://github.com/luffysolution-svg/omnischolar/tree/HEAD/skills/zotero-research) · ★8)
- **zotero-research**: Isolated Zotero-corpus research worker. Use through the host delegation mechanism to discover or verify bounded claims and return multi-source evidence cards with immediate verbatim passages, qualifications, contradictions, and search receipts. Never searches for, fetches, or imports external sources. ([ccam80/thesis-writer](https://github.com/ccam80/thesis-writer/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/thesis-writer/skills/zotero-research) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **zotero-research**: Busca en la biblioteca local de Zotero (y fuera de ella, en OpenAlex/Crossref), genera citas en formato CSL (APA, MLA, Chicago, Harvard, IEEE, Vancouver) y mantiene actualizada la sección de bibliografía de documentos Markdown y Word. - **zp-judge**: 遇到“要不要改／要不要做／该继续还是停”这类决定，尤其证据少、样本小或自己已经倾向某个答案时使用：先判断这件事成不成立、值不值得做，再钉清决定与证据，对“做”和“不做”两边都先让反方说满，再让支持方说满，最后给出改、不改或再观察的结论、翻转条件和一个最小实验。用户说“帮我判断”“要不要改”“这个数据怎么看”“先唱个反调”时调用。数据少时不得只说“再等等”，必须同时给出能拿到数据的具体动作。问题本身还说不清时先用 zp-question；人生方向层面的选择交给 zp-life，本技能管具体决定的判断方法。 ([ZanePan2027/zpskill](https://github.com/ZanePan2027/zpskill/tree/HEAD/skills/zp-judge))

- **zp-k8s**: ZeroProtocol hunter for externally-reachable Kubernetes and container platforms - anonymous API-server access, the read-only kubelet on 10255 versus the full kubelet on 10250, exposed etcd, token-less dashboards, and ingress that misroutes between namespaces or tenants. - **zp-laravel**: ZeroProtocol hunter for Laravel and PHP application footguns - .env and APP_KEY exposure, debug mode and Ignition, Telescope, Horizon and Debugbar dashboards, mass assignment through fillable and guarded, replayable or unsigned signed URLs, Eloquent raw-query injection, docroot and storage-disk exposure, unencrypted cookies and loose-comparison auth bypass. - **zsh-word-splitting-fails-the-edit-and-the-grep-that-checks-it**: zsh 는 `$var` 를 단어 분할하지 않는다 — `FILES=$(find ...)` 뒤 `sed ... $FILES` 는 목록 전체를 **파일명 하나**로 넘겨 15개 치환이 전부 실패한다. 진짜 위험은 실패 자체가 아니라, 바로 뒤 검증 `grep ... $FILES` 가 **같은 관용구를 공유해 함께 죽는 것**이다: 편집도 안 되고 검증도 "잔여 없음"처럼 보인다. 배열 `FILES=(...)` + `"${FILES[@]}"` 를 쓰고, 검증은 편집과 **다른 방식으로 대상을 지정**하라. 트리거 - zsh 에서 여러 파일 일괄 치환, `$(find)`/`$(grep -l)` 결과를 명령에 전달, 치환 후 grep 으로 확인, "No such file or directory" 에 목록 전체가 찍힐 때. ([blas1n/claude-skills](https://github.com/blas1n/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/zsh-word-splitting-fails-the-edit-and-the-grep-that-checks-it) · ★2)
